We pray on this day of your Vyāsa-pūjā that we may be able to follow in your footsteps and become worthy of your association by faithfully carrying out your instructions.

Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your eternal servants in the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda temple, Calcutta, India.

## Canberra

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances! Please allow us to surrender at your lotus feet. All glories to Śrī Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga! All glories to your transcendental ISKCON! All glories to the devotees who have joined through your transcendental mercy in the form of your books and lectures! All glories to your wonderful disciples who have dedicated their lives for spreading your mission to save the whole world!

Another year has passed, and your movement has continued to expand everywhere. Here in Canberra we have continued our service and tried to keep the goal steadily in view, that is, to have your Society accepted as part of the tapestry of society at large and to make sure it is widely known that your ISKCON movement is the institution that is saving the world through its temples, *prasādam* distribution, preaching, and book distribution. Our Centennial offering to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is to have your temple under construction before the end of this year on the land granted to us by the government. Also, on all fronts we wish to improve our endeavors to make your ISKCON a grand success.

The success of the movement is due to the purity of the devotees who are serving your desire, so please bless us so we can become instrumental in achieving this goal. Please help us become pure devotees so your movement can become a grand success. We are begging to remain your surrendered souls. Please empower us to continue serving your mission in whatever capacity we can.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!!!

Your servants in Canberra, Australia.

## Cape Town

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your most beautiful lotus feet. All glories to the 100thanniversary of your birth!

The devotees at the ISKCON temple in Cape Town, South Africa, are feeling your presence very much during this very auspicious year. Your lotus feet are a source of all benedictions and complete shelter within this world. You made that mercy so easily available by allowing yourself to be installed in your *mūrti* form in ISKCON centers around the world. You give everyone the opportunity to advance in spiritual life by associating with your devotees and reading your books and following your instructions. While we preach here in Cape Town, we try to glorify you by allowing your instructions to penetrate our hearts so that we can then try to penetrate the hearts of others with those instructions by your mercy. Actually, we cannot accomplish such penetration: we can only beg that you allow the penetration process to take place.

Our temple here in Cape Town is engaged in a lot of university preaching. Therefore we have a lot of students in the temple. You said that university preaching was one of the most important preaching programs because if students take to Kṛṣṇa consciousness then they can influence the higher echelon of society. So with complete faith in these words of yours we are maintaining a steady program of activities centered on Kṛṣṇa consciousness, including vegetarian catering of *prasādam* and the opening of a temple room on campus. In this room students can come and take refuge from their mundane studies and relax their minds by chanting and hearing Lord Kṛṣṇa’s holy name.

Some have called this city of Cape Town one of the most beautiful cities on this planet. Therefore we have to always try and remember you so that we do not become bewildered by the glare of *māyā* here. You also have given your mercy so fervently to many of the inhabitants of this city. One of your disciples, His Holiness Bhakti-bhṛṅga Govinda Swami, commented that everybody here in Cape Town chants Hare Kṛṣṇa. That is truly due to your mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please allow us to glorify you this year by doing some much-needed temple renovation and finally paying off our debts. I know this will please you very much. For the sacrifice you performed in the course of delivering *kṛṣṇa-prema* on behalf of the most munificent incarnations, Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, we, your humble, menial servants, owe you an eternal debt. We understand your exalted worshipable position as the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON, but even more than that we know that you came directly form Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s paradise, Goloka, the supreme abode.

May we always have the opportunity to preach your glories and message. May we always remember and never forget our eternal constitutional position as atoms in the dust of the Supreme Personality of Godhead’s lotus feet. May we never offend your disciples, their disciples, your followers, or anyone in general. Please help us remain pleasant and undisturbed, always fixed in the reality of the relationship between the *jīvātmā* and Paramātmā.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue giving us your mercy, and kindly excuse our many faults that have caused our long delay in trying to fulfill your many transcendental desires for this city.

Your humble servants in Cape Town, South Africa.

## Caracas

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We inaugurated the Centennial year by making a pilgrimage to East Park, “Parque Del Este,” where you walked every morning while you visited our Caracas temple. A busload of people came from a far-away town just to participate in the pilgrimage. Some of our Godbrothers came, many local devotees too. We took your *mūrti* on the morning walk, with *kīrtana* accompanying the procession; we stopped at every spot where you performed a pastime or gave a lesson.

We had mixed feelings in our hearts. As we remembered your pastimes and teachings, so many were eager to know about you—some for the first time. Others, who already knew your books and had visited the temple regularly, increased their attachment for you and their appreciation of your movement.

We went clockwise, as you did. A lawyer had asked you about the scarcity of food in the world, and you had preached to him showing him a small bird eating fruit on a branch of a tree. You explained how Kṛṣṇa is maintaining all living entities.

By your mercy we are trying to write a book about your *līlā* and teachings in Caracas, and we hope that this effort will please you, because this is the goal of our life. Your pleasure and satisfaction are all that we want. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for allowing us to serve you despite our imperfections and mistakes.

Your servants in Caracas, Venezuela.  
(written by Jagad-cakṣu dāsa)

## Chicago

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet, which are purifying to all the world!

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

We take transcendental delight in hearing your glorification on this Centennial anniversary of your divine appearance. Throughout the three worlds those who are fortunate are hearing of you, the greatly empowered representative of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Our own Centennial offering is very insignificant. We therefore offer the remaining years of our meager lives as our Centennial offering. Please accept this inadequate substitution.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

So rnuch is happening as your ISKCON movement expands. It is amazing to witness and participate in this very real drama of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Your sincere followers push on the preaching work, and then Māyā attacks in some form or fashion There’s a struggle. Māyā is winning! No, the devotees are winning … no, Māyā advances … devotees … Māyā … then Kṛṣṇa appears! Victory!

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

We want to become really sincere servants of Your Divine Grace. When we look closely at ourselves, we realize there is so much cleansing of the heart to be done. But we love you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we know that that love can transform steel-framed hearts We have seen it done!

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

In this Centennial year, we have some requests that we want to humbly submit:

1 ) Please give us unflinching dedication to your mission. (That also requires our correct understanding of your mission.) You told us that this dedication is the secret to success, and you personally demonstrated this fact.

2\) Please give us the ability to successfully execute your mission. Success or failure is not the central point, but we want to offer you positive results for your pleasure.

3\) Please keep us in the association of your sincere and faithful followers.

Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*!

We beg to remain  
Your servants in Chicago, Illinois, USA.

## Colombo

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this occasion of your most auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day, we, your humble servants, offer our repeated obeisances unto your lotus feet. Please shower your mercy on us so that we can remain under the shadow of your lotus feet forever.

By the grace of Lord Kṛṣṇa, one hundred years ago you appeared on this planet to deliver the fallen conditioned souls, and when you appeared it was predicted that you would open 108 temples around the world. And Your Divine Grace made that prophecy come true.

Five hundred years ago Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu predicted that in every town and village His name and glory would be sung. To execute the orders of your Guru Mahārāja and fulfill this prophecy, Your Divine Grace went to the West to preach. And Your Divine Grace made that prophecy come true.

Also, Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura predicted that Americans, Germans, Russians, and many others from all over the world would come to Śrī Māyāpur to do *saṅkīrtana,* and Your Divine Grace made that prophecy also come true.

By your mercy the whole world will become Kṛṣṇa conscious, and that was your desire. Your desires to serve Kṛṣṇa are unlimited because you are a pure devotee empowered by the Lord to deliver the fallen conditioned souls. We are fortunate in this life to have seen you and received your mercy.

On your way to America onboard the *Jaladuta,* Your Divine Grace stopped at the Colombo harbor in Sri Lanka and drove in the Colombo city the day before Janmāṣṭamī. We are fortunate that you have visited Sri Lanka before you went to America. We are sure that Sri Lanka will also become Kṛṣṇa conscious by your mercy, even though Sri Lanka is known as the land of Rāvaṇa. We pray for your mercy that we in Sri Lanka get your blessings to be in your service by spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Your most fallen servants in Colombo, Sri Lanka.

## Columbus

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

We would like to take the opportunity, on this most auspicious occasion of your appearance day during your Centennial Celebration, to thank you for giving us shelter in this humble temple, where we can best learn to serve you, your devotees, and one another. We consider ourselves very fortunate to be so abundantly blessed by the association of your disciples, who help us understand what it means to live under the protection and shelter of your instructions. It is by their inspiration that we aspire to raise our consciousness and our standards during this Centennial Year.

In a revolutionary preaching spirit, you allowed women to join your preaching forces, and through your divine vision and mercy we in Columbus are now aspiring to spread your glories, your mission, and the chanting of the holy names. This Centennial Celebration is an opportunity to increase our consciousness of you and to strengthen, reunite, and expand your family. Yet the effort to accomplish these goals will not finish with the close of this year. May our service to you always increase and improve. We humbly take shelter in the shade of your lotus feet, under the guidance of your faithful representatives, and beg that by your mercy we will be fully engaged as your menial servants, birth after birth.

Your aspiring servants in Columbus, Ohio, USA.  
(written by Sītāpriyā-devī dāsī)

## Copenhagen

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances prostrated in the dust of your lotus feet, which are forever moving to deliver the conditioned souls to the lotus feet of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Residing at Rādhā-Dāmodara,  
Peaceful you were in Kṛṣṇa’s land,  
The perfect home for devotees,  
Not far from Yamunā’s bank.

Simple living,  
Absorbed in devotion,  
Living with Kṛṣṇa—  
Your natural position

Looking out your kitchen,  
You got inspired  
By Rūpa’s *samādhi.*  
Spiritual communion transpired.

You were writing your books,  
Preparing for your life’s mission,  
A revolution never before seen,  
Beginning with your translation.

You were not there to retire  
But to prepare  
To fulfill your *guru’s* order,  
Which you had nurtured with care.

Whereas others would never leave,  
You ventured forth,  
Braving the ocean,  
Fighting against all odds.

Armed with faith in Kṛṣṇa,  
You arrived at America’s shore.  
Praying for the Lord’s mercy,  
You fervently called

To a culture whose youths were lost.  
You reached out to touch their souls,  
Attracting them by your sincerity,  
Your preaching forceful and bold.

With no resources,  
Nor institutional help,  
You just chanted and spoke—  
Kṛṣṇa’s presence would be felt.

Many a time  
You thought of going back,  
But remembering your *guru’s* order  
Stirred you onwards to act.

By your firm faith and devotion  
You thus built a house  
To give shelter to all  
And to our dormant love arouse.

In every town and village  
Gaurāṅga’s name will be chanted,  
Fulfilling His prediction.  
The seeds have been planted

A saint among saints,  
A true emissary of the Lord,  
An emblem of love and compassion—  
Following you will take us back to God.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
For coming  
And, despite great difficulties,  
Not returning.

We beg for the strength to move in time with your swift-moving lotus feet.

Your eternal servants at New Śrīvāsāṅgam, Copenhagen, Denmark.  
(written by Cirañjīva dāsa)

## Czech Farm and Govinda’s Restaurants

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Every year the blessed event of the anniversary of your transcendental appearance in this world is an occasion to take stock of all our realizations in the matter of your matchless gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You have given the world this matchless gift, and for those who have received it, it completely changed not only their philosophical outlooks but above all their very lives.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! You always exemplify the perfectly Kṛṣṇa conscious person. Your *vapu* form may not be visible to our mundane eyes, but your eternal *vāṇī* form can be seen, felt, and realized by all those sincere devotees who regularly read your books, listen to your tapes, watch your classes on ITV videos, translate, edit, publish, and distribute your books, worship your *mūrti,* or manage the various centers of your ISKCON society or your BBT editing facilities. Having day by day realizations of your divine presence means that we have become at least to some extent Kṛṣṇa conscious, because it is a fact that *there is no Kṛṣṇa consciousness without realizing your presence in every aspect of our* sādhana. All of our realizations are but a part and parcel of your causeless mercy. Śrīla Prabhupāda! *You are that unique person who sustains kṛṣṇa consciousness in the hearts of all members of ISKCON*. Without your divine inspiration, we would all be in *māyā:* all of our activities would be nothing less and nothing more than hopeless illusions.

On this auspicious Centennial anniversary of your transcendental appearance in this world, we pray that we will never forget that you are always present in your *vāṇī* form, you are actually with us at every step, you never left us, and you will never leave us.

*cakhu-dān dilo jei, janme janme prabhu sei,*  
*divya-jñān hṛde prokāśito*

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Your servants at the Czech Farm and Govinda’s Restaurants.  
(written by Jaya Gurudeva dāsa)

## The Czech Republic

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Another year is over, and now it’s time to offer our homage to you on this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

Even though we could not meet you when you were physically present on this planet, we can realize your presence when we simply devote ourselves to the service of your bona fide representatives, our spiritual masters, who follow in your footsteps and tirelessly travel on the surface of this planet to make your mission well known to each person in every town and village.

Your sincere disciples are coming from everywhere, bringing your books, your mood, and your desires, and by their example and by teaching what they have learned from you, they spread your mercy to the next generation of your followers.

You have given everything needed to go back to our full Kṛṣṇa consciousness, to reawaken our eternal relationship with Kṛṣṇa. Therefore we simply want to understand, follow, and spread your mission “as it is,” according to your scheme

“Books are the basis.  
Preaching is the essence.  
Utility is the principle.  
Purity is the force.”

We realize that if we stick to these principles, cooperating closely with other devotees, then we are protected by you and you are with us, giving us your mercy. You are within your books, and by distributing them we can realize that it is not just a phrase but a fact.

We can see that people who come in contact with your books become enlivened because they are spiritual souls who have been hankering for this spiritual food for many lifetimes. You are feeding the whole planet. Your books are food for the real self, food full of eternity, bliss, and knowledge. Since you came to the West, millions of people have been and are being reminded of Kṛṣṇa. They honor your books, your life, your self. More and more people are bowing down to your lotus feet every day.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have already given us everything, but still we beg you to help us become your dedicated servants and expand this *saṅkīrtana* movement for your pleasure and for the benefit of suffering humanity.

Please protect us from all kinds of deviations and give us the intelligence and strength to present your teachings in the right way, without adding our own impurities.

Your aspiring servants in the Czech Republic.

## Dallas

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances.

We have approached you and inquired about the Supreme Absolute Truth. Your responses to our inquiry are worthy because they relate to the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa and so are of relevance to the world’s welfare. Such answers are capable of completely satisfying our souls.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are definitely a follower in the footsteps of Lord Caitanya and His associates, for you have also plundered the storehouse of love of God and freely distributed its contents to everyone who came in contact with your person or your books.

You gave us the supreme occupation for all humanity, by which everyone can attain to loving service to the transcendental Lord.

You’ve given us occupational activities which provoke attraction to hearing your message of the Personality of Godhead.

Away from sense gratification you have guided us and made us desire only a healthy life meant for inquiry into the Absolute Truth.

We can only realize the Supreme Lord by rendering devotional service in terms of what we have heard from you or what we read in your glorious books.

Your conclusion about the highest perfection we can achieve is to please the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa. Therefore with great power and enthusiasm you constantly spoke about, glorified, and demonstrated how to worship the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa in order that we may remember and worship Him, who is the protector of devotees like yourself.

You have armed us with the sword of knowledge. Intelligently we can now cut through the binding knots of material existence. Therefore if people are wise, they should give great attention to your message about the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are a pure devotee who is completely freed from vice. By serving you, indeed, great service is done. By such service one gains affinity for hearing the messages of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

By our regularly hearing from you and reading your books, all that is troublesome to our hearts is almost completely destroyed and loving service to Śrī Kṛṣṇa is established as an irrevocable fact.

You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have established this irrevocable loving service to the Supreme Lord in our hearts. Thus the effects of passion and ignorance, such as lust, desire, and hankering, are disappearing from our characters.

By your grace we are becoming enlivened, and we look forward to greater realizations—realizations that will keep us enthusiastic to continue our preaching efforts to spread this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your servants in Dallas, Texas.

## Debrecen

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances in the holy dust of your all-merciful lotus feet. All glories to your appearance, which has enlightened this dark Age of Kali. All glories to Lord Caitanya’s ever-victorious *saṅkīrtana* movement, which by your mercy will soon be spread to every town and village on the surface of the earth and even beyond!

This year is the year of your Centennial Celebration. This is truly the most significant event in modern history. It has never happened before and will never happen again. We must consider ourselves most fortunate to be able to participate in this event. We promise to do our best to spread your glories far and wide.

I was thinking about those people who had the opportunity to meet you personally, to receive instructions from you, or to become your disciples. Although when joining the movement and even later on we are instructed to meditate on you as our *śikṣā-guru,* I still want to experience what they feel for you. Now, when you are not manifest before our blunt senses, it’s only faith that can help us establish a relationship with you. And this faith comes mostly from those who have associated with you, your disciples.

I’m trying to imagine: if I suddenly met you, what would you be like? What would you say? And what would I say to you? If you suddenly rang the doorbell of our small temple here in Debrecen (which, actually, very much resembles the Matchless Gifts storefront), what would happen?

Devotees would begin to run up and down: “Śrīla Prabhupāda is here! Hey, bring him a garland! Bring the *vyāsāsana*!” Devotees would cheerfully sing, “*Jaya* Prabhupāda! *Jaya* Prabhupāda!” You would appear in the door, smiling and looking upon the devotees .Ladies and small girls would run up to you with flowers in their hands, and you would distribute them to the devotees. You would enter the temple room and slowly offer your prostrated obeisances before the painting of the Pañca-tattva. Then you would sit on the *vyāsāsana*. Everyone would crowd into the temple room and fall silent. You would pick up a pair of *karatālas* and slowly start to sing “Jaya Rādhā-Mādhava.” You would close your eyes and sing the lines over and over again with increasing intensity while slightly rocking your head. Then you would finish and chant the *prema-dhvani*. You would open the *Bhāgavatam* and have a devotee reading a *śloka* and a purport. Then you would start to speak.

Everyone sits and listens as you explain one philosophical point after the other, giving lively examples, sometimes joking, sometimes slightly raising your voice while completely smashing all the materialistic ideas of this dark Age of Kali. Absorbing yourself in thoughts of Kṛṣṇa, you tell a story about Him and His devotees. Your voice is warm and slightly emotional because of your feelings of ecstasy. You powerfully call us to surrender to Kṛṣṇa and chant His holy name and spread His glories through the world.

The picture slowly fades away. You are no longer with us. Still, you are with us! In your books, in your tapes, in your pictures, and especially in the hearts of your dear disciples you are eternally with us. I have read once that when you were asked to say something about Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, you answered, “What can I tell you? He was a Vaikuṇṭha man!” I think this is also true for you. While looking at my spiritual master, His Holiness Śivarāma Mahārāja, who has spent almost the last ten years establishing Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Hungary, I feel the greatness of your personality and your missionary vision manifesting in him. He is truly empowered by you, and this is because he is completely yours. I dream that some day, by his mercy, we will also become your *nija-jana,* your own men, your army that is ready to conquer the whole world of illusion and bring everyone back to Kṛṣṇa’s *mādhurya-dhāma,* Goloka Vṛndāvana, your eternal home. We wish to remain always sheltered at your lotus feet.

Your servants in Debrecen, Hungary.  
(written by Gaurāṅga dāsa)

## Denver

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances on this most auspicious day of your Mahā-Vyāsa-pūjā.

As we struggle to glorify the fathomless depths of your devotion to Kṛṣṇa and your immense Kṛṣṇa conscious contributions to this world, we remember your response when one of your disciples asked you to speak of your spiritual master. You said, “What can I say about him? He was a Vaikuṇṭha man.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are a Vaikuṇṭha man, and your miracle is that you are turning hundreds and thousands into Vaikuṇṭha men and women by your instructions. These instructions have spread the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mantra* all over the world, have already established 450 temples around the world, and are quickly ushering in a ten-thousand-year golden age of Kṛṣṇa consciousness into this world. Śrīla Prabhupāda, your instructions are fully empowered by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

These instructions are our access to intimate association with you and Kṛṣṇa, and are therefore the very essence of life for your sincere followers.

In Denver we vigorously try to adhere to your instructions. We insist that everyone living in the *āśrama* attend the full morning program, chant sixteen good rounds, and follow the regulative principles. We study your books, and distribute as many as possible. We distribute large quantities of delicious *prasādam* through Govinda’s Buffet, your Sunday feast program, and our recently expanded Hare Kṛṣṇa Food for Life. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are increasing the family members, as many new devotees are joining us. We are also “boiling the milk” by working on our relationships and helping each other come closer to you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are trying to worship and glorify you by strictly following your instructions. We know that because of our rendering this *guru-vāṇī-seva* you will empower us to give your mercy to others.

Thank you for all that you have given us.

Your servants in Denver, Colorado, USA.

## Detroit

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

All glories to you, Param Pujya Abhay Charanaravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda, who have established the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement by extending the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to each and every living entity on earth.

All glories to you, the eternal resident of Śrī Śrī Māyāpur Chandrodaya Mandir, who fulfilled the desire of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura by propagating Lord Kṛṣṇa’s name and fame through *harināma-saṅkīrtana,* book distribution, *prasādam* distribution, and the opening of temples all around the world.

All glories to you, dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, who came to America to shower your mercy on the souls of the Western world, who, being covered by the material modes of ignorance and passion and being fully absorbed in material life, have no taste for the transcendental message of Vāsudeva.

All glories to you, the eternal resident of Śrī Śrī Krishna-Balaram Mandir, who always prayed to Lord Kṛṣṇa so that ignorant souls like us could be convinced by the message of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Bhagavad-gītā* and understand the mellows of devotional service.

All glories to you, O Śrī Deva-Sadan Nivāsi, who always understood the will of the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa and who always prayed and desired for the release of the bound souls from the clutches of illusion by making them understand Lord Kṛṣṇa’s message.

All glories to you, O spiritual master of the world, who always took the humble position as a devotee and at the same time showed the power of a devotee by engaging thousands of disciples in the service of Lord Kṛṣṇa and changing the lives of millions of people just by repeating the message of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

All glories to you, the savior of the universe, who’s words were always pure and who’s lectures and instructions thus penetrated the hearts of many fortunate souls, who then became liberated from all unhappy conditions of life.

All glories to you, ‘O’ divine son of Śrī Gour Mohan De, who always remained like a puppet in the hands of Lord Kṛṣṇa and danced and sang the glories of Lord Śrī Caitanya all around the world fourteen times and made millions dance with you.

All glories to you, O Jagadguru Śrīla Prabhupāda, without whom we would never have any knowledge of, faith in, or devotion for Lord Kṛṣṇa, and because of whom we have Lord Jagannātha’s Ratha-yātrā in all major cities of the world.

All glories to you, deliverer of the devotees at the Deva-sadan Mandir. It is only with your loving care and protection, your mercy and guidance, your books and *prasādam,* your smiling form on your *vyāsāsana* and your “Your ever well-wisher” at the end of every letter are we devotees at the Deva-Sadan Mandir able to continue serving your very dear Deities, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kuñjabihāri, Śrī Śrī Jagannātha Swami, Baladevji, and Subhadra-mātā, and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi and Lord Śrī Nāthji

All glories to you, O Śrīla Prabhupāda. We would like to pray to you to hold us tight in your lap, just as a father holds his children during a hurricane, as we have a tendency to run away form your shelter without knowing the dangers of the material world.

Your unworthy servants at the Deva-Sadan Mandir in Detroit, Michigan, USA.  
(written by Jagadguru dāsa)

## Dresden

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you.

The books of this world are filled with the stories and realizations of many great personalities. Parents, friends, and teachers told us about great revolutionists, saints, and philosophers. Sometimes we felt bored by all of this, sometimes threatened, sometimes excited. But it never touched us deeply. Never.

Rather, in any state of life we felt the touch of “the sand in the sweet rice. ” We tried our best to enjoy, but it never worked to our full satisfaction. But what was the alternative? Just another kind of sand. Some sand may be not as horrible in the sweet rice than another kind of sand, but sand is sand. Pure sweet rice seemed to be unavailable. Great personalities seemed to have died out.

Your compassion with us Westerners, crazy “sand-hunters, ” is still beyond our capacity to fully grasp. You gave us a way to make sense of our lives, persons we could love and who love us, a mission, friends, the best food, the best music, the best philosophy, the holy name; you gave us guidance and shelter, you gave us the spiritual world.

You gave us Kṛṣṇa. It is not an empty slogan to declare that no one is able to give more than that.

We know that words of thankfulness and appreciation, however carefully and expertly chosen, won’t impress you very much. You expect us to show our gratitude by taking all that you have given us and giving it to others. Your gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness has to become the most important part of our lives, and we have to help others take it up too.

We assure you that we will try our best, although spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness is not always easy. Our “sand consciousness” haunts us constantly, but we pray that we will never forget where the real nectar in life is to be found: in the taste of pure (sweet-rice–like) Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

May your glories spread all over the three worlds!

Your insignificant servants in Dresden, Germany.  
(written by Diviratha dāsa)

## Durban

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

As the founder-*ācārya* of ISKCON, you taught every aspect of Kṛṣṇa consciousness by your own perfect example and in your books. To achieve success we must follow as you have instructed us.

“Following the order of the bona fide spiritual master is the only duty of the disciple, and this completely faithful execution of the order of the bona fide spiritual master is the secret of success.” (*Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* 2.9.8)

With complete faith and conviction you executed the instructions of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura to preach the message of Lord Caitanya all over the world. You followed the principles, especially as explained by Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura, that the duty of a disciple is to strictly follow the order of his spiritual master. The success of your ISKCON is based on the principle of strict faith in the order of the spiritual master.

When you visited Durban, South Africa, in October 1975, you gave your disciples two specific instructions. One was to build a magnificent temple in the heart of the Indian community, and the second was to hold Ratha-yātrā festivals in Durban. Your disciples, granddisciples, and followers took these two instructions as their life and soul in fulfilling your desires. In 1985 the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānātha Temple was opened. Ratha-yātrās and festivals have been held regularly since 1977, and in 1988 the first annual Festival of the Chariots was held on the Durban beachfront.

These projects have attracted the attention of the leaders and people of South Africa. The president of South Africa, Dr. Nelson Mandela, who is also the Nobel Peace Prize winner and one of the world’s leading statesmen, made a state visit to the temple in 1994. He has met devotees regularly and accepted your books and *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* on numerous occasions. The Deputy President, Mr. Mbeki, was the chief guest of honor at our Ratha-yātrā festival in 1995 and served *prasādam* to the people at the festival. Dr. Bhutelezi, Minister of Home Affairs, who officially opened the temple in 1985, visited it again as a guest of honor during the tenth anniversary celebration in 1995. Many national and provincial government ministers, as well as diplomats and community leaders, have also visited the temple and attended our various festivals over the past three years. The leaders of the city of Durban have recognized the temple as an important landmark and the Ratha-yātrā festival as one of the highlights of the city’s annual festival attractions. The leaders of the Hindu community have always supported your ISKCON and assisted in every way possible to help establish the movement. Whenever we faced any threat, they stood at the forefront to protect ISKCON. Although the leaders disagree on so many issues, they all agree on the fact that your ISKCON and the all the activities you gave us contribute in a very important way to uplifting the people of South Africa.

ISKCON has developed into and is recognized as one of the most important organizations in South Africa. This has happened only by your mercy. By our becoming faithful and fixed in Your Divine Grace’s instructions, your preaching here will continue to be successful.

We must become completely fixed on the eternal and fundamental principle of strictly following your instructions and fulfilling all your desires. Then we will obtain the highest perfection in life.

We beg to remain  
Your obedient disciples in Durban, South Africa.

## Gadei Giri

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

When you came to Bhubaneswar and performed the cornerstone laying ceremony for the Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma temple on the appearance day of Lord Nityānanda (February 2, 1977), that was a very big year, a very sacred year, because that year Lord Jagannātha accepted a new form, *nava-kalevara*. Your Divine Grace remained in Bhubaneswar for more than two weeks, in a small mud hut built by your dear disciple Gaura-Govinda Swami. You also started translating the Tenth Canto of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* at that time.

Every day hundreds of people came and joined your disciples in listening to you speak, and in spite of financial stringency, on your instruction His Holiness Gaura-Govinda Swami arranged distribution of *prasādam* to all during the entire time that Your Divine Grace was in Bhubaneswar.

After leaving Bhubaneswar you fell ill and went to Vṛndāvana. When Gaura-Govinda Swami went to see you in Vṛndāvana, you mentioned that you would like to go to Bhubaneswar again. But your senior disciples did not think you should go to Bhubaneswar in that condition; so Gaura-Govinda Swami came back to Bhubaneswar, and after some time you left the planet.

From that time on, Gaura-Govinda Swami devoted all his time to fulfilling your desire to build the Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma temple in Bhubanesvar. He wanted to make it a wonderful project in your honor. By your mercy many fallen souls joined the center in Bhubaneswar and became instruments in the hands of Gaura-Govinda Swami, trying to help him fulfill your desire that the preaching mission in Orissa, of which Bhubaneswar is the capital, should flourish.

Gaura-Govinda Swami did not engage the devotees simply in collecting money for the construction of the temple; rather, he always tried to develop our strong faith in *sādhu, guru,* and *śāstra* through his nectarean *kṛṣṇa-kathā*. So all the devotees are bound up by his love and affection and especially his deep realization of the *śāstra* and his surrender to your mission.

Within a period of nineteen years, despite so many hurdles, he finished the temple in Bhubaneswar, which was your cherished desire. When almost all the work had been finished, on the very special day February 9, 1996, at 6:40 p.m., on the appearance anniversary of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura Prabhupāda, in Śrīdhām Māyāpur, you took him to the abode of Kṛṣṇa in order to take part in *nitya-līlā rasa* with Your Divine Grace. Perhaps Gaura-Govinda Swami felt he had not had enough association with Your Divine Grace during the three years that you were on the planet after he had met you, and so, now that the temple had been completed, he may have felt it more important to be in your presence in Goloka Vṛndāvana.

We have heard that when Your Divine Grace ordered Gaura-Govinda Swami to go to Bhubaneswar you told him to depend on Kṛṣṇa because a Vaiṣṇava should not feel helpless. Now you have given us the same teaching by taking away your dearmost disciple untimely. Now, we know, we should depend on Kṛṣṇa. But Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are not the devotee His Holiness Gaura-Govinda Swami is; we are very neophyte. So how can we tolerate the departure of a real *sādhu* like Gaura-Govinda Swami, our beloved Guru Mahārāja, who kept us together to spread your movement all over the world? So many times our Guru Mahārāja would quote Your Divine Grace, “Tolerate and co-operate.” We have nothing left but this instruction—“Tolerate and co-operate.” We need strength, and we believe that by your mercy we can tolerate this darkness that has appeared, now that the *sādhu* has left the planet.

It is interesting to note that our Guru Mahārāja left this world in a year similar to the one that saw the disappearance of Your Divine Grace: in this year, as in 1977, Lord Nityānanda’s appearance day is February 2, and this year also Lord Jagannātha’s Nava-kalevara ceremony is taking place. (Lord Jagannātha’s Nava-kalevara is not a regular occurrence.)

Your servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopāl Jiu Temple in Gadei Giri, Orissa.  
(written by Caitanya-candra dāsa)

## Gītā-Nāgarī

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Thank you for giving us the most sublime engagement of mass distribution of your books. Through this activity we feel connected to you and your beloved Guru Mahārāja. This process awakens love for Kṛṣṇa.

*tattva-vastu—kṛṣṇa, kṛṣṇa-bhakti, prema-rūpa*  
*nāma-saṅkīrtana—saba ānanda-svarūpa*

**“The Absolute Truth is Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and loving devotion to Śrī Kṛṣṇa exhibited in pure love is achieved through congregational chanting of the holy name, which is the essence of all bliss.”** ***Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi-līlā*** **1.96)**

Book distribution inspires us to live the following verse:

*tṛṇād api sunīcena taror iva sahiṣṇunā*  
*amāninā mānadena kīrtanīyaḥ sadā hariḥ*

**“One should chant holy name of the Lord in a humble state of mind, thinking oneself lower than the straw in the street; one should be more tolerant than a tree, devoid of all sense of false prestige, and should be ready to offer all respects to others. In such a state of mind one can chant the holy name of the Lord constantly.”**

This verse explains the means and the end of book distribution. Through being meek, humble, tolerant, and ready to offer all respect to others, we are able to impress others that Kṛṣṇa consciousness is a very good thing. If we behave like this, then it will be much easier to absorb ourselves in chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra*. If we don’t pay attention to our chanting, how can we expect others to pay attention to us? This service of book distribution forces us to chant very attentively so that we can deal properly with our own minds and so that we can handle the various opposing elements on *saṅkīrtana* in a sublime way.

Book distribution teaches us to offer all respect to others. In America people are generally quite proud and arrogant on one side and jaded by so many offers on the other. If we truly offer all respect to others by seeing Kṛṣṇa in their hearts, we will move even the most hard-core American. The *Bhāgavatam* praises seeing Kṛṣṇa in everyone’s heart as being the best method of spiritual enlightenment.

**“Indeed, I consider this process—using one’s mind, words and bodily functions for realizing Me within all living beings—to be the best possible method of spiritual enlightenment.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **11.29.19)**

So you have kindly given us this service of distributing your books that is “the best possible method of spiritual enlightenment.” I consider the mean, nasty, arrogant, and impolite people we meet on book distribution as manifestations of your mercy. Through them Lord Caitanya is training us to be more tolerant than a tree.

You told us in a famous letter to the New Dwaraka community that by dedicating our lives to distributing your books we are guaranteed to go back to Godhead. But only if we become pure can we do this. Please help us chant purely, read purely, and see Kṛṣṇa in everyone’s heart. Please awaken love in our hearts for Śrī-Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara.

Your aspiring servants at the Gītā-Nāgarī farm in Pennsylvania, USA.  
(written by Vijitātmā dāsa)

## Gothenburg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

As I write this I realize I never before wrote a Vyāsa-pūjā offering to Your Divine Grace. After all, I am not your direct disciple but your granddisciple. So it is a bit unusual to address you like this. But at the same time it feels like I know you—and of course my Guru Mahārāja has made me understand how you are also my spiritual master. You are like a loving grandfather, and as my Guru Mahārāja explained, “Sometimes the grandfather is more merciful to the children than father is.” So as granddisciples we can also feel that closeness to you. And we can learn to really appreciate your wonderful qualities and teachings.

Of course, that is not very difficult. Your transcendental qualities are unlimited. In fact, every day we realize more and more about the wonderful and astonishing influence you are having on this world and our lives. And among all your opulences, your preaching spirit is the most wonderful one.

When struggling here, we sometimes become despondent when the obstacles seem too big, and at that time we may remember how you also struggled for forty to fifty years without an apparent success. You tried several ways to preach and fulfill the instructions of your Guru Mahārāja, but actually the result was not really there—or so it may have appeared. You tried in Jhansi and in Calcutta, sending letters to influential persons, trying to mobilize a preaching mission among the Gauḍīya Math. But there was not much response. Anybody else would have lost his determination. What to speak of when you reached the age of seventy? It would even have seemed justified to shrug your shoulders and say, “Well, I tried my best,” and retire to Vṛndāvana.

But you did that last thing, that thing which may even have seemed desperate or even irresponsible at such an advanced age: you crossed the ocean, traveling alone to the West, with no money, no sponsors, nothing—except a few books.

You did that thing. And then Kṛṣṇa gave you everything—a lifetime of preaching that changed the world and saved thousands, no, *millions* of lives in just a few years.

I have a hard heart. But I have to confess that every time I think about this sacrifice of yours my heart melts and tears fill my eyes. This alone is enough to keep me going, struggling to serve my Guru Mahārāja in his service to you, preaching in this desert of Kali-yuga. And who knows? Maybe Kṛṣṇa will also some day send thousands and millions of souls who are eager to hear the message of the Lord, and we will drown in the ocean of the nectar of preaching.

In this Centennial year, several of us devotees here have had the same experience, namely that we have been pushed by Kṛṣṇa to surrender more, to give up all nonsense, to chant better *japa,* to study your books, to straighten out our temple management and get into more preaching. As a matter of fact, the push is strong! So we see all this as your special mercy. We are not getting any younger, and we should not think the game is over just because we are middle aged. It may just be the beginning.

And again, when I look at your picture on my wall, you seem so close when I think like that.

Your dāsānudāsas in Gothenburg, Sweden.

## Hartford

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances.

On this most auspicious occasion of the hundredth anniversary of your divine appearance in this world, we again dedicate ourselves to you and offer ourselves in the dust of your divine lotus feet. It is your inconceivable mercy that you are still here for us: teaching us, helping us, guiding us at every moment. When we do take shelter of you, we do realize this. And this realization is also only by your mercy.

Our understanding of your glories, your immeasurable accomplishments, and your overwhelming and perfect love for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa only increases with the passage of time. Only though our attachment to you and through our desire to somehow, in some tiny way, help to fulfill your transcendental desires will we someday be saved from the material cycle of birth and death.

We pray that you will please not kick us away but will continue to rain your mercy on us. It is the only way in which we may someday make you proud to have us as your disciples and granddisciples.

Your eternal servants in Hartford, Connecticut.

## Heidelberg

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the divine dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you and your sincere followers.

One hundred years ago on this most auspicious day, Mother Earth became blessed by the touch of your lotus feet, and she celebrated the appearance of the most munificent person who has ever touched her surface.

Although Gour Mohan De, your fortunate father, was a wonderful devotee of Kṛṣṇa and a *pukka* Vaiṣṇava, who could have imagined that his son would become the savior of the whole world? Shortly after your auspicious appearance, people could see what a nice Vaiṣṇava you are.

Even in childhood you were conducting Ratha-yātrā festivals and performing attractive dramas in glorification of the Supreme Lord. All this was done in such a wonderful way that it would bring tears to the eyes of the onlookers.

When on that afternoon in 1922 you visited your celebrated spiritual master, Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Mahārāja, who could have thought that on that day the history of mankind had come to a turning point? You have accepted the mission of your spiritual master as your heart and soul and thereby showed us the proper example in regard to our duty toward the conditioned souls.

As we go through the towns and villages of this world, we observe the precarious condition of the people. We see how they are very unhappy, being constantly cheated by politicians and scientists who misuse their votes and trust, making big promises of a great future and happy life but factually forcing people to live in horrible conditions in the name of “freedom.” As we ourselves were mostly born in materialistic families, we know how meaningless it is to lead a life of sense gratification and mental speculation, constantly dragged by the mind and senses.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you came to us empowered by the supreme Lord, with the weapon of knowledge in your hands, and saved us from our miserable condition of life. We are very grateful to you.

You have performed many austerities in order to attract us to Kṛṣṇa. Before you, no one had ever left the sacred land of Bhārata-varṣa to mercifully preach to others about the glories of the Supreme personality of Godhead. All the so-called swamis and gurus who went out of India were interested in their own name and fame. But you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, were not in the same category. You did not leave India to for a sightseeing tour of America, as some less intelligent people thought! You were not thinking of fame, profit, and adoration. You did not go there to live in material opulence. You had a mission—to spread the glories of the Lord, which you have done in a most perfect manner, under the direct order of your Guru Mahārāja.

As we speak with different people, telling them about you, we see that often they’ve never heard about you. In fact, we are now trying to make you known to the people, for not so many people know you. It may seem amazing, but although many people know the Hare Kṛṣṇas, hardly anyone has ever heard about the founder-*ācārya* of this movement. One can easily understand this, for you never tried to make yourself famous, as did the bogus *gurus*. You made Kṛṣṇa famous. Lord Kṛṣṇa is full in all opulences (*ṣaḍ-aiśvarya-pūrṇa*), including fame. Still, you made these opulences known to the people of this world.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please be merciful to us and allow us to remain inspired in this most important *saṅkīrtana* mission. Please let us not be misguided by materialistic attractions and self-centered ideas. Please focus our attachments on your mission. Thank you very much.

Your servants in Heidelberg, Germany.

## Helsinki

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories unto you, by whose mercy the eternal wisdom of Kṛṣṇa consciousness has been brought even to the furthest corners of this world. When you left Vṛndāvana-dhāma for New York in 1965, you didn’t know what to expect. You had been instructed to preach in the English-speaking countries by Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, and so you were going to do it. “Somehow or other, now, my Lord, it is up to You to make me a success or failure, as You like.”

In the Gauḍīya Math you were never considered a great leader or a pioneer in fulfilling Lord Caitanya’s prophecy that His holy name would be chanted in all the towns and villages in the world. But upon going to the West you took the position of a highest kind of leader. “I have hundreds of temples and disciples; only time is separating me from them,” you told an subway conductor who saw you sitting alone playing *karatālas*.

Immediately after sowing *bhakti-latā* seeds in the hearts of a few Americans and opening a couple of centers around in the States, you thought about the rest of the world: Europe, Asia, and Russia. “My disease is that I can never think small,” was your humble and humorous comment to your amazed disciples who couldn’t keep up with you. You had come to save the world, so what was to be your limitation? “‘Impossible’ is a word in a fool’s dictionary,” you taught your doubting followers. You showed us an example of a first-class disciple by fulfilling 100% of your Guru Mahārāja’s order and even expanding beyond his instruction.

For almost fifteen years your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement has been working in Finland. Most of your books have been translated and are being slowly but surely distributed around the country. *Prasādam* distribution has increased during the last few years, and hundreds of pieces of *prasādam* are going out daily. Street *harināmas* should be increased—we pray for your mercy so we can tolerate the freezing climate. Every month we hold an India Experience program in each big city, and Finnish folks show their spiritual inclinations by visiting and demanding more lectures. Sometimes we meet people who declare that they only read your books and are eagerly buying more to complete their Bhaktivedanta set.

We are starting to realize that this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is a truly revolutionary movement. Marching in a demonstration or writing rebellious slogans on city walls is peanuts; the real revolution is to understand we are Kṛṣṇa’s eternal servants and act accordingly.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are eternally thankful to you. You are the greatest general in Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* army, and because of your purity and determination Kṛṣṇa has sent you many sincere followers, who now act as captains, admirals, and commanders for us granddisciples. And although we didn’t see you personally, we don’t have to lament. You are like Kṛṣṇa in the sense that words spoken by you or about you are ever fresh and keep us connected eternally.

Your humble servants in Helsinki, Finland.  
(written by Tarkalī-devī dāsī)

## Hong Kong

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this most auspicious day.

All glories to Your Divine Grace. All glories to your Centennial Celebration. All glories to your dedicated followers.

We are preaching in a city embedded in an ancient culture with deep-rooted materialistic ethics. However, Your Divine Grace once told us that preaching means to turn the unfavorable into favorable. So, Śrīla Prabhupāda thank you very much for giving us such a challenging preaching field—so much so that each of us can have plenty of services day and night that keep us immune from the influence of *māyā*. And this, your Centennial Celebration, will be a turning point for all our preaching programs.

*kirāta-hūṇāndhra-pulinda-pulkaśā*  
*ābhīra-śumbhā yavanāḥ khasādayaḥ*  
*ye ’nye ca pāpā yad-apāśrayāśrayāḥ*  
*śudhyanti tasmai prabhaviṣṇave namaḥ*

**“Kirātas, Hūṇas, Āndhras, Pulindas, Pulkaśas, Ābhīras, Śumbhas, Yavanas, members of the Khasa races and even others addicted to sinful acts can be purified by taking shelter of the devotees of the Lord, due to His being the supreme power. I beg to offer my respectful obeisances unto Him.” (*****Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **2.4.18)**

Everyone is eligible to receive Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, even the Khasa races; they are no exception, provided we let your unlimited mercy flow. This means we have to communicate, coordinate, and cooperate among ourselves. Sometimes we have to accommodate and come to a consensus in order to bring the fallen, conditioned souls closer to Kṛṣṇa. As you emphasized in your last days, “Your love for me will be shown by how much you cooperate to spread this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.” Hong Kong was blessed three times by the touch of your lotus feet during your earthly *līlā*. Everything will be manifested in course of time because the sweet will of a pure devotee never fails.

It is now up to us to dedicate ourselves to your mission with our bodies and souls.

Your disciples and granddisciples in Hong Kong.

## Imphal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

Every day, when the sun sets in the west, we feel that the date of the Centennial Celebrations for Your Divine Grace is drawing very near. Our only hope is Your Divine Grace. We have many things to do in your divine mission, but we are unable to accomplish all. Your auspicious instructions to His Holiness Bhaktisvarūpa Dāmodara Swami Śrīpāda Mahārāja—“Make the Manipur temple like Navadvīpa and Vṛndāvana”—is the only solace of all of us in Manipur. Though we have no good qualities, we are trying our best to fulfill your desire by building the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇacandra-maṇi Mandir at the ISKCON Manipur Complex in Imphal. With your instructions and blessings, we are sure that all of us in Manipur will be able to complete the construction of the ISKCON complex at Imphal very soon to fulfill the desire of Your Divine Grace. We feel most fortunate taking shelter in your divine mission.

We humbly beg you to engage us in executing our devotional service to the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇacandra, following the instructions of Your Divine Grace.

Your unworthy Servants in Imphal, Manipur, India.

## Kerala

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, thank you for visiting this earth planet and giving us a whiff of the spiritual world. We see you repeatedly in your books and your preaching movement, and in our dear spiritual master, who is a repository of instructions of Your Divine Grace. Simply by associating with him and many of your dear disciples, we see what you have wanted and what you have been while you walked on this planet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda you are an ocean of mercy and the shelter of all living entities.

There are few of us here endeavoring to push on your mission, albeit ineffectively, in the state of Kerala, which you visited to send your books by ship before you went to the West. Kerala, the state with the highest literacy rate in India, the birthplace of Śaṅkarācārya and the hotbed of politics, communism, and intellectual rigidity, has already gotten your mercy in the form of your books. Our efforts are repeatedly challenged by Māyāvādīs and impersonalists, but the innocent and faithful are always available to preach to.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your pleasure we acquired a wonderful piece of land very close to the main temple in Guruvayur—the most important *tīrtha-sthāna* in Kerala and the next most popular to Tirupati. Śrīla Prabhupāda, with your special blessings we are daring to attempt to construct a preaching center in the heart of Guruvayur, for distributing your literature and *prasādam* to the lakhs of pilgrims who come there. At present we see a lot of opposition and petty problems shrouding our endeavors. But we are sure that we will be successful and that from here your voice will be heard loud and clear all over the bastion of the Māyāvādīs and impersonalists. In this way you will drive away the witches haunting their minds, so they will come crawling on their knees asking for your mercy.

We pray at the dust of your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you bestow on us, your grandchildren, the strength and intelligence to pursue devotional service and drown the land of Lord Paraśurāma in *harināma-saṅkīrtana* and devotion to Kṛṣṇa from the base of this grand preaching center in Guruvayur, which is to be dedicated to Your Divine Grace.

Your servants in Kerala, India.

## Laguna Beach

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Your life is light. Your words are thunder.  
The great *ācārya* we bow under.  
Preachers can talk but never teach,  
unless they practice what they preach.

Eternity too short a time to chant your glories so sublime,  
guiding us with the light you left to shine,  
like a star on high that exists no more,  
it’s light continuing to shine for years on score.

One on Kṛṣṇa’s side you are the majority that surmounted.  
Devotion to Kṛṣṇa made you grow unbounded.  
Your nectarean words were weighed not counted,  
measured by depth not inches you expounded.

Good, better, best, you never let it rest,  
until your good was better and your better best.  
Now everyone can attest you past Kṛṣṇa’s test,  
the greatest *ācārya* to come to the West.

Kṛṣṇa prepares great men by great trials, it’s true.  
Many austerities you underwent to bring Kṛṣṇa’s message through.  
Like a piece of coal under extreme pressure diamonds ween.  
Adversity Kṛṣṇa’s university it’s deemed.

No need to curse the darkness of little use.  
Light our candles by knowledge you infuse.  
No question for those who believe these truths.  
No answer for those who refuse your news.

Our road to material success was always under construction,  
spending time that simply led to our destruction.  
Investing time for the divine your greatest instruction.  
Serving your lotus feet is the only sane injunction.

We swam in sin, we sank in sorrow,  
our outlook in Laguna wasn’t worth a dollar.  
You showed us the in-look by reading your books,  
now happiness, an inside job, by your grace we’re hooked.

Immediate payment for serving Kṛṣṇa may not be seen,  
But the retirement plan is out of this world, it seems.  
Dependability on you is our greatest ability.  
Those who believe are the ones who achieve this stability.

Your devotees in Laguna Beach, California, USA.  
(written by Vyāpi dāsa)

## Lautoka

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Child Trying to Touch the Moon**

Auspicious birth, blessed saw,  
Spell of Kali had others in ignorant snore.  
Unnoticed on earth, child is Godsent,  
Heavens celebrated the momentous event.

Bhaktisiddhānta ignited fire to preach,  
Abhay discussed Kṛṣṇa, whom he could reach.  
Family not inclined, will not co-operate,  
Godbrothers had *maṭhas* to operate.

Desire to serve, *guru’s* order on his head,  
Present in English words heard, studied, and read.  
No support, resources, you have to do alone  
In old age, daring, fearless, true to his name.

Sacrifice, glory, and deeds be broadcast  
Far and wide for generations to hear.  
Prabhupāda journeyed on *Jaladuta*  
To save the unfortunate of Kali-yuga.

Revolutionary, adjusted norms few ever dared.  
*Bhakti* is core for women, *mlecchas—*none spared.  
Devotion is transcendental, everyone’s right.  
Spread Mahāprabhu’s teachings for His delight.

Suffering entities, Vaiṣṇava cannot bear.  
Impersonal realization—“Kṛṣṇa is not there.”  
Transcendental shelter, ISKCON to set you free.  
Chanting, dancing, feasting, cross the *virajā* sea.

Our words insignificant, meager, atomic specks.  
Makes us happy to offer appreciation and respects.  
A hundred years since your divine birth,  
Untold sent to eternal *dhāma,* freedom from death.

You, towering high, we concede defeat,  
Like child trying to touch moon from his seat.  
You are not an entity of ordinary nature.  
Impossible to glorify a person of your stature.

Distribute books, co-operate, preach, and serve.  
Saint’s active order all can have.  
Your love is teaching, chastising, guiding forever.  
You are always our ever well-wisher.

Your eternal servants in Lautoka, Fiji.

## Liverpool

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

On this truly auspicious day,  
your Vyāsa-pūjā day,  
we hear your many glories  
and unlimited nectarean stories.

Your pastime which is most glorious,  
as it makes everyone victorious,  
is the reclaiming of fallen souls  
who have blindly lost their goals.

Lost in *māyā’s* mazes,  
we go through many phases.  
This dark and hazy place  
is illuminated by your grace.

You shine the way for all.  
Still we tremble and we fall.  
You are our only hope;  
you throw us a golden rope.

That rope is your instructions,  
which save us from destruction.  
Caring father—Your Divine Grace,  
saving the whole human race.

You encourage us to be bold.  
You encourage us to take hold  
of the golden rope you give  
as you show us how to live.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray  
to remember you every day  
with an open heart and mind;  
then to Kṛṣṇa we’ll be inclined.

Please help us to truly hear  
So we will have no fear.  
Your teachings so strong and bold—  
your golden rope so firm to hold.

Your golden rope has many strands;  
it’s broad enough for many hands.  
It can weave through our hearts,  
tearing *māyā’s* plans apart.

By hearing about you we become purified;  
associating with you pushes *māyā* aside.  
This day is truly your mercy as well.  
Your compassion has saved us all from hell.

Your aspiring servants in Liverpool, England.

## Ljubljana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Our dear grandfather spiritual master, please accept our prostrated obeisances in the precious dust of your compassionate lotus feet, and please also accept this small attempt to glorify you on your especially auspicious Centennial Vyāsa-pūjā Day.

**Śrīla Prabhupāda’s Determination and Courage**

“What is hell?” the journalist asked.  
“London is hell,” Prabhupāda said.  
“But we have come here  
To teach you  
What you have forgotten.  
And we are not afraid  
Of your big civilization.  
Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta sent me,  
And now I will do it,  
No matter what.  
I will not wear your three-piece suit,  
I will not eat meat or drink liquor,  
And you will soon wear *dhotī* and *tilaka,*  
Eat *prasādam*,  
And chant Hare Kṛṣṇa.”

“Where will you get the money?”  
“You will give it.  
Kṛṣṇa will tell you and you will give.”

And so it was.

**Śrīla Prabhupāda’s Challenge**

“The Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement  
is for training men to be independently  
thoughtful and competent in all types of  
departments of knowledge and action.”

The opportunity one can get for releasing his potential and creativity in Śrīla Prabhupāda’s Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is tremendous and amazing. Therefore this movement of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s is a great challenge for all kinds of experts and other gifted people, who should join ISKCON and do something wonderful and lasting in their lives.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we get your mercy through your unbreakably determined and faithful followers, our spiritual masters. Please let us go on studying your books under their guidance and serving them in this exciting preaching mission. In this way let us attain excellence in all kinds of activities and relationships. You always wanted that members of ISKCON become perfect gentleman and men of ideal character. Please help us make your ISKCON movement as attractive and loving as you are, but also as strong and uncompromising as you are. Thus your ISKCON will become an irresistibly perfect example for the whole world.

The aspiring servants of your servants in Ljubljana, Slovenia.

## London (South)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Śrīla Prabhupāda Centennial kī jaya!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who at an advanced age departed from the glorious land of Vṛndāvana to create the Vṛndāvana atmosphere all around the world.

One hundred years ago, the day after His appearance, Lord Kṛṣṇa showered upon the unfortunate souls of Kali-yuga a grace rarely ever bestowed in the world of mortals. He sent us His very dear one, whose absence, even for a moment, He can hardly tolerate. Out of His unlimited compassion He gave us you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Five hundred and ten years ago, when Kṛṣṇa Himself appeared as Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu, He promised that one day the whole world would be delivered by the chanting of His holy names. Many times in the past the Lord has personally intervened to fulfill the promise of His devotees. but in this case the Lord entrusted to His pure devotee the rare privilege of fulfilling His own promise.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have saved the world from the destructive ocean of Kali-yuga. A verse the sages at Naimiṣāraṇya spoke to Śrīla Sūta Gosvāmī applies very nicely to you:

*tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā dustaraṁ nistitīrṣatām*  
*kaliṁ sattva-haraṁ puṁsāṁ karṇa-dhāra ivārṇavam*

**“We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being.”** ***(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.1.22)**

Like Mahārāja Parīkṣit, you boldly challenged the personality of Kali. Out of great fear he has recoiled by ten thousand years. By your uncommon *brahma-tejas,* you have reversed the trend of the times. With the spreading of Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* through your ISKCON, a new Satya-yuga is dawning, nested within an otherwise degraded and turbulent age. Although your physical presence on this planet was relatively brief, you have irreversibly changed the lives of thousands of people and will continue to affect millions more in the centuries to come. By your expert presentation of *bhāgavata-dharma* you are gradually bringing about “a cultural revolution for the re-spiritualization of the entire human society.”

By your mercy, that cultural revolution has had a significant effect on our lives, and we are trying to please Your Divine Grace by our insignificant efforts. Kindly bless us in order that our attempts to please you and your disciples becomes successful, so that Lord Kṛṣṇa will cast His merciful glance upon us.

Your insignificant granddisciples in south London, England.

## Longdenville

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you on this supremely auspicious festival of your Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, occurring today exactly one hundred years since your all-merciful appearance.

If in this homage we were to attempt to describe the magnitude of your contribution to this world and to our own lives, we could certainly go on for another one hundred years. And still that one hundred years would not be sufficient to enunciate all your glories. But what better engagement could we have than to make the attempt? For if you had not come to save us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, what would we be doing today instead of gathering at your lotus feet to offer you homage, praise, and thanks?

Certainly we would have been living excruciatingly boring lives. Lives full of hellish sinful activities assuring our demotion to lower species for millions of births. Lives full of pain, anguish, and bewilderment. Lives filled with attachment to family, friends, home, society, nation—thinking them the all in all. Lives full of emptiness—a dim reflection of real existence. These were our lives before you touched them. Perhaps at times we felt an inkling that there must be more than this painful, dull existence, but who knew where to look for it?

But then, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you entered our lives, breaking through the thick fog of illusion like the bright effulgent sun. The warm rays of your love for Kṛṣṇa shone through our cold material coverings and touched the cores of our hearts. You picked us up from an ocean of despair, confusion, and hopelessness that was life before, and you showed us real life. You gave us the holy name, the association of pure devotees, worship of the Deity, temples to associate and serve in, transcendental knowledge in the form of your books, lectures, and personal instructions, a mission to engage our restless bodies and minds in practical devotional service. You gave us the full mercy of Lord Gaurāṅga. Most of all, you gave us your most kind and loving self. Everything we know and experience in Kṛṣṇa consciousness is intimately connected with you and simply reminds us of you. You gave us everything we have, and your own life is outstanding proof of the reality of what you gave. You gave your whole life to give us life, to show us our real identities as Kṛṣṇa’s servants, to guide us and coax us back to our eternal living relationship with the Supreme Lord. You ensured that our once dismal futures are now bright, blissful, and all-auspicious if we only stay with you.

Thank you, thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. How can we begin to express out gratitude to you sufficiently? We have a debt to you that we can never finish repaying. Only a fool thinks that he can repay the debt to the spiritual master.

Yet as there is no better engagement for us on this day than trying to glorify you although we can barely begin to do so, similarly there is no better way to spend the entirety of our existence than in trying to repay our debt to you.

Our prayer is that not only on this Vyāsa-pūjā day but on every day of our lives we may simply be absorbed in dedicating ourselves to serving your lotus feet. There is no reality beyond this. No life. We fervently pray to always mold our lives according to your desires. To always be able to hear and chant your glories in the association of your devoted followers. To make pleasing you the only goal of our lives.

Although the homage we offer you today is inadequate and unworthy of you, we try to offer these words with whatever love and sincerity we have. When one offers Ganges water to Mother Gaṅgā, Mother Gaṅgā does not gain anything, but the worshiper is benefited. Whatever we now have in our lives—indeed, our very life itself—was given to us by you, and you gain nothing by our offering it back to you. Still we beg to offer you everything we have today and throughout our lives. You are the most dear and confidential devotee of the Lord, having come to deliver pure *kṛṣṇa-prema* to the most unqualified fallen *jīvas*. Thus we are fully confident that there is no higher perfection than to take shelter in the dust of your lotus feet eternally.

Praying to remain  
Your servants at the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha Mandir, Longdenville, Trinidad.

## Madras

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāminn iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept at your lotus feet the humble obeisances of your spiritual grandchildren at ISKCON Madras. By the mercy of the bona fide spiritual masters that you have given us, empowered with your mercy, we are linked to the *guru-paramparā* and are able to contemplate writing a humble Vyāsa-pūjā offering to Your Divine Grace.

Not a day that passes without our thanking Your Divine Grace for the wonderful house you have built for the whole world to live in. When, following your orders to preach, we meet the suffering people, we recollect how we ourselves were suffering but were saved by Your Divine Grace’s tireless efforts to set up this organization. Śrīla Prabhupāda, though we never saw you when you manifested your pastimes here personally, still we never feel far from you, for by your mercy you have left yourself in your books, from reading which we derive the greatest pleasure, and in the memory of those who had so much personal association with you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you wrote in a letter to the early preachers in South India (Śravaṇānanda and Bhāvabhūti Prabhus): “In Madras we have to construct a very gorgeous temple. … Never mind what the cost will be. We are not concerned with the amount of money, but we want a very attractive temple.” You gave another instruction in the letter to Śrī Bhāvabhūti, “These atheists class of men \[members of a political party\] should be converted into theism by preaching *Bhagavad Gita*. ”

It is mentioned in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* how the people of Satya-yuga will take birth in Kali-yuga on the banks of Tāmraparṇī and Kāverī rivers to participate in the *saṅkīrtana-yajña*. So, this land that we are trying to serve you in is very special.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have left several orders for us. How can we fulfill these without your divine mercy? We are trying our best. Now we await your mercy. You have mercifully given us the guidance of your disciple, His Holiness Bhānu Swami Mahārāja, as our temple president and regional secretary. We are gearing up to finally start on the temple project. Please give us your mercy and inspiration to fulfill your transcendental orders.

On the occasion of this very special Vyāsa-pūjā that marks the centennial anniversary of your appearance, we pray for your spiritual presence to guide us and push us day by day. We hope also to have your mercy so we may be more dedicated to our spiritual masters, who are serving you most sincerely and faithfully.

Your granddisciples in Madras, India.  
(written by Sumitra-Kṛṣṇa dāsa)

## Madrid

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances at the dust of your divine lotus feet.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have tried to be enjoyers, controllers, and lords of this material dream for many lives, like children who build sand castles on the beach and after awhile see them destroyed by the force of time and tide.

After many lives of disappointment, perhaps we haven’t learned enough to really desire and look for the eternal shelter of your divine lotus feet. So we try to relieve our difficulties in the fallible shelters of family, friends, fame, money, and false ego. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us the intelligence to abandon everything unreal that has infiltrated our hearts and that is preventing us from serving you and your desires with all of our hearts.

After many lives of envy, desires for power, greed, and pride, we aren’t satisfied yet, and in our devotional life we constantly envy your willing servants—our brothers and sisters—and all the other living entities.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us your mercy so that we can love and be compassionate toward all living beings.
